Ogievetsky-Shlosman conjecture: If $\omega>1+\sqrt{2}$, then $(3,\omega)\in\PCyl$
This certificate proves: If $\omega>\frac{91}{37}$, then $(3,\omega)\in\PCyl$. 
The numbers $\frac{97}{31}$ and $1+\sqrt{2}$ have the same first decimal digit.
